Save metrics\n    with open('/root/metrics.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Check constraints\n    print(\"\\nConstraint Verification:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error < 0.5C: {steady_state_error < 0.5} ({steady_state_error:.4f}C)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time < 120s: {settling_time < 120} ({settling_time:.4f}s)\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot < 10%: {overshoot < 0.10} ({overshoot*100:.2f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature < 30C: {max_temp < 30.0} ({max_temp:.4f}C)\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"TEMPERATURE CONTROLLER SYSTEM\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # IMC tuning rules for first-order system\n    if K > 0:\n        Kp = tau / (K * lambda_c)\n        Ki = 1.0 / lambda_c\n    else:\n        print(\"Warning: Invalid K value, using default gains\")\n        Kp = 10.0\n        Ki = 0.1\n        lambda_c = 10.0\n\n    Kd = 0.0  # Save tuned gains\n    with open('/root/tuned_gains.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(tuned_gains, f, indent=2)\n\n    return tuned_gains\n\n\ndef run_closed_loop_control(sim, tuned_gains, setpoint=22.0, duration=150.0, calibration_end_time=0.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run closed-loop temperature control.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance (already at calibration end state)\n        tuned_gains: Dictionary with Kp, Ki, Kd\n        setpoint: Target temperature (C)\n        duration: Control duration (seconds)\n        calibration_end_time: Time offset from calibration phase\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with control log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nRunning closed-loop control to {setpoint}C for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    controller = TemperatureController(\n        Kp=tuned_gains[\"Kp\"],\n        Ki=tuned_gains[\"Ki\"],\n        Kd=tuned_gains[\"Kd\"]\n    )\n\n    dt = sim.get_dt()\n    data = []\n\n    # Run control loop\n    steps = int(duration / dt)\n    for i in range(steps):\n        # Get current temperature (from last step or initial)\n        if i == 0:\n            # We're continuing from calibration, so step once to get current state\n            result = sim.step(0.0)\n        else:\n            # Compute control action\n            error = setpoint - current_temp\n            heater_power = controller.compute(error, dt)\n\n            # Apply control action\n            result = sim.step(heater_power)\n\n        current_temp = result[\"temperature\"]\n        current_time = calibration_end_time + result[\"time\"]\n        error = setpoint - current_temp\n\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": round(current_time, 2),\n            \"temperature\": round(current_temp, 4),\n            \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n            \"heater_power\": round(result[\"heater_power\"], 4),\n            \"error\": round(error, 4)\n        })\n\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    # Save control log\n    with open('/root/control_log.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Control complete: {len(data)} data points\")\n    final_temp = data[-1][\"temperature\"]\n    final_error = abs(final_temp - setpoint)\n    print(f\"Final temperature: {final_temp:.4f}C (error: {final_error:.4f}C)\")\n\n    return control_log\n\n\ndef calculate_metrics(control_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate performance metrics from control log.\n\n    Args:\n        control_log: Dictionary with control data\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating performance metrics...\")\n\n    data = control_log[\"data\"]\n    setpoint = control_log[\"setpoint\"]\n\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n    errors = np.array([d[\"error\"] for d in data])\n\n    # Initial temperature\n    T0 = temps[0]\n\n    # Rise time: time to go from 10% to 90% of setpoint change\n    delta_T = setpoint - T0\n    T_10 = T0 + 0.1 * delta_T\n    T_90 = T0 + 0.9 * delta_T\n\n    idx_10 = np.where(temps >= T_10)[0]\n    idx_90 = np.where(temps >= T_90)[0]\n\n    if len(idx_10) > 0 and len(idx_90) > 0:\n        rise_time = times[idx_90[0]] - times[idx_10[0]]\n    else:\n        rise_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Overshoot: maximum excursion beyond setpoint\n    max_temp = np.max(temps)\n    if max_temp > setpoint:\n        overshoot = (max_temp - setpoint) / abs(delta_T) if abs(delta_T) > 0 else 0.0\n    else:\n        overshoot = 0.0\n\n    # Settling time: time to stay within 2% of setpoint\n    settling_band = 0.02 * abs(delta_T)\n    settled = np.abs(errors) <= settling_band\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        # Find first time it enters and stays in band\n        first_settled_idx = np.where(settled)[0][0]\n        # Check if it stays settled\n        if np.all(settled[first_settled_idx:]):\n            settling_time = times[first_settled_idx] - times[0]\n        else:\n            # Find last time it enters the band permanently\n            for i in range(len(settled) - 1, -1, -1):\n                if np.all(settled[i:]):\n                    settling_time = times[i] - times[0]\n                    break\n            else:\n                settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n    else:\n        settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Steady-state error: average error over last 10% of data\n    steady_state_window = int(len(temps) * 0.1)\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(np.abs(errors[-steady_state_window:]))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"rise_time\": round(rise_time, 4),\n        \"overshoot\": round(overshoot, 6),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 6),\n        \"max_temp\": round(max_temp, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Rise time: {rise_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Overshoot: {overshoot*100:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"Settling time: {settling_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Steady-state error: {steady_state_error:.4f} C\")\n    print(f\"Max temperature: {max_temp:.4f} C\")\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open('/root/metrics.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Check constraints\n    print(\"\\nConstraint Verification:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error < 0.5C: {steady_state_error < 0.5} ({steady_state_error:.4f}C)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time < 120s: {settling_time < 120} ({settling_time:.4f}s)\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot < 10%: {overshoot < 0.10} ({overshoot*100:.2f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature < 30C: {max_temp < 30.0} ({max_temp:.4f}C)\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"TEMPERATURE CONTROLLER SYSTEM\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Step 4: Run closed-loop control\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"STEP 4: CLOSED-LOOP CONTROL\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    control_log = run_closed_loop_control(\n        sim,\n        tuned_gains,\n        setpoint=22.0,\n        duration=150.0,\n        calibration_end_time=calibration_end_time\n    )\n\n    # Proportional term\n        P = self.Kp * error\n\n        # Integral term with anti-windup\n        self.integral += error * dt\n        I = self.Ki * self.integral\n\n        # Derivative term\n        D = 0.0\n        if dt > 0:\n            D = self.Kd * (error - self.prev_error) / dt\n        self.prev_error = error\n\n        # Compute output\n        output = P + I + D\n\n        # Clamp output to valid range\n        output = np.clip(output, 0.0, 100.0)\n\n        # Anti-windup: prevent integral windup\n        if output >= 100.0 or output <= 0.0:\n            self.integral -= error * dt\n\n        return output\n\n\ndef run_calibration(sim, heater_power_test=50.0, duration=35.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run step test for system identification.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        heater_power_test: Step input power (%)\n        duration: Test duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with calibration data\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running calibration test with {heater_power_test}% heater power for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Extract time series\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n\n    # Initial temperature (average of first few points to reduce noise)\n    T0 = np.mean(temps[:min(5, len(temps))])\n\n    # Ambient temperature (assume initial temp is ambient)\n    T_ambient = T0\n\n    # First-order step response model:\n    # T(t) = T_ambient + K*u*(1 - exp(-t/tau))\n    def first_order_response(t, K, tau):\n        return T_ambient + K * u_step * (1 - np.exp(-t / tau))\n\n    # Initial parameter guesses\n    # K: roughly (final_temp - initial_temp) / u_step\n    # tau: time to reach 63.2% of final value\n    delta_T = temps[-1] - T0\n    K_guess = delta_T / u_step if u_step > 0 else 0.1\n    tau_guess = 30.0\n\n    try:\n        # Fit the model\n        params, covariance = curve_fit(\n            first_order_response,\n            times,\n            temps,\n            p0=[K_guess, tau_guess],\n            bounds=([0.01, 1.0], [1.0, 200.0]),\n            maxfev=10000\n        )\n\n        K_est, tau_est = params\n\n        # Calculate R-squared\n        y_pred = first_order_response(times, K_est, tau_est)\n        ss_res = np.sum((temps - y_pred) ** 2)\n        ss_tot = np.sum((temps - np.mean(temps)) ** 2)\n        r_squared = 1 - (ss_res / ss_tot) if ss_tot > 0 else 0.0\n\n        # Calculate fitting error (RMSE)\n        fitting_error = np.sqrt(np.mean((temps - y_pred) ** 2))\n\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_est, 6),\n            \"tau\": round(tau_est, 4),\n            \"r_squared\": round(r_squared, 6),\n            \"fitting_error\": round(fitting_error, 6)\n        }\n\n        print(f\"Estimated K = {K_est:.6f} C/%\")\n        print(f\"Estimated tau = {tau_est:.4f} s\")\n        print(f\"R-squared = {r_squared:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"Fitting error (RMSE) = {fitting_error:.6f} C\")\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Parameter estimation failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Using fallback parameter estimates\")\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_guess, 6),\n            \"tau\": 30.0,\n            \"r_squared\": 0.0,\n            \"fitting_error\": 1.0\n        }\n\n    # Save estimated parameters\n    with open('/root/estimated_params.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(estimated_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    return estimated_params\n\n\ndef calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=1.5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate PI controller gains using IMC tuning rules.\n\n    IMC tuning for first-order system:\n    Kp = tau / (K * lambda)\n    Ki = 1 / lambda\n\n    Args:\n        estimated_params: Dictionary with K and tau\n        lambda_factor: IMC filter time constant multiplier\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with controller gains\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating controller gains using IMC tuning rules...\")\n\n    K = estimated_params[\"K\"]\n    tau = estimated_params[\"tau\"]\n\n    # IMC tuning parameter (desired closed-loop time constant)\n    # lambda should be chosen based on desired speed vs robustness\n    # Larger lambda = slower but more robust\n    lambda_c = lambda_factor * tau\n\n    # IMC tuning rules for first-order system\n    if K > 0:\n        Kp = tau / (K * lambda_c)\n        Ki = 1.0 / lambda_c\n    else:\n        print(\"Warning: Invalid K value, using default gains\")\n        Kp = 10.0\n        Ki = 0.1\n        lambda_c = 10.0\n\n    Kd = 0.0  # No derivative action for first-order system\n\n    tuned_gains = {\n        \"Kp\": round(Kp, 6),\n        \"Ki\": round(Ki, 6),\n        \"Kd\": round(Kd, 6),\n        \"lambda\": round(lambda_c, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Kp = {Kp:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Ki = {Ki:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Kd = {Kd:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Lambda (filter time constant) = {lambda_c:.4f} s\")\n\n    # Save tuned gains\n    with open('/root/tuned_gains.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(tuned_gains, f, indent=2)\n\n    return tuned_gains\n\n\ndef run_closed_loop_control(sim, tuned_gains, setpoint=22.0, duration=150.0, calibration_end_time=0.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run closed-loop temperature control.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance (already at calibration end state)\n        tuned_gains: Dictionary with Kp, Ki, Kd\n        setpoint: Target temperature (C)\n        duration: Control duration (seconds)\n        calibration_end_time: Time offset from calibration phase\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with control log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nRunning closed-loop control to {setpoint}C for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Compute control action\n            error = setpoint - current_temp\n            heater_power = controller.compute(error, dt)\n\n            # Apply control action\n            result = sim.step(heater_power)\n\n        current_temp = result[\"temperature\"]\n        current_time = calibration_end_time + result[\"time\"]\n        error = setpoint - current_temp\n\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": round(current_time, 2),\n            \"temperature\": round(current_temp, 4),\n            \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n            \"heater_power\": round(result[\"heater_power\"], 4),\n            \"error\": round(error, 4)\n        })\n\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    # Initial temperature\n    T0 = temps[0]\n\n    # Rise time: time to go from 10% to 90% of setpoint change\n    delta_T = setpoint - T0\n    T_10 = T0 + 0.1 * delta_T\n    T_90 = T0 + 0.9 * delta_T\n\n    idx_10 = np.where(temps >= T_10)[0]\n    idx_90 = np.where(temps >= T_90)[0]\n\n    if len(idx_10) > 0 and len(idx_90) > 0:\n        rise_time = times[idx_90[0]] - times[idx_10[0]]\n    else:\n        rise_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Overshoot: maximum excursion beyond setpoint\n    max_temp = np.max(temps)\n    if max_temp > setpoint:\n        overshoot = (max_temp - setpoint) / abs(delta_T) if abs(delta_T) > 0 else 0.0\n    else:\n        overshoot = 0.0\n\n    # Settling time: time to stay within 2% of setpoint\n    settling_band = 0.02 * abs(delta_T)\n    settled = np.abs(errors) <= settling_band\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        # Find first time it enters and stays in band\n        first_settled_idx = np.where(settled)[0][0]\n        # Check if it stays settled\n        if np.all(settled[first_settled_idx:]):\n            settling_time = times[first_settled_idx] - times[0]\n        else:\n            # Find last time it enters the band permanently\n            for i in range(len(settled) - 1, -1, -1):\n                if np.all(settled[i:]):\n                    settling_time = times[i] - times[0]\n                    break\n            else:\n                settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n    else:\n        settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Steady-state error: average error over last 10% of data\n    steady_state_window = int(len(temps) * 0.1)\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(np.abs(errors[-steady_state_window:]))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"rise_time\": round(rise_time, 4),\n        \"overshoot\": round(overshoot, 6),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 6),\n        \"max_temp\": round(max_temp, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Rise time: {rise_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Overshoot: {overshoot*100:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"Settling time: {settling_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Steady-state error: {steady_state_error:.4f} C\")\n    print(f\"Max temperature: {max_temp:.4f} C\")\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open('/root/metrics.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Check constraints\n    print(\"\\nConstraint Verification:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error < 0.5C: {steady_state_error < 0.5} ({steady_state_error:.4f}C)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time < 120s: {settling_time < 120} ({settling_time:.4f}s)\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot < 10%: {overshoot < 0.10} ({overshoot*100:.2f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature < 30C: {max_temp < 30.0} ({max_temp:.4f}C)\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"TEMPERATURE CONTROLLER SYSTEM\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Ambient temperature (assume initial temp is ambient)\n    T_ambient = T0\n\n    # First-order step response model:\n    # T(t) = T_ambient + K*u*(1 - exp(-t/tau))\n    def first_order_response(t, K, tau):\n        return T_ambient + K * u_step * (1 - np.exp(-t / tau))\n\n    # Initial parameter guesses\n    # K: roughly (final_temp - initial_temp) / u_step\n    # tau: time to reach 63.2% of final value\n    delta_T = temps[-1] - T0\n    K_guess = delta_T / u_step if u_step > 0 else 0.1\n    tau_guess = 30.0\n\n    try:\n        # Fit the model\n        params, covariance = curve_fit(\n            first_order_response,\n            times,\n            temps,\n            p0=[K_guess, tau_guess],\n            bounds=([0.01, 1.0], [1.0, 200.0]),\n            maxfev=10000\n        )\n\n        K_est, tau_est = params\n\n        # Calculate R-squared\n        y_pred = first_order_response(times, K_est, tau_est)\n        ss_res = np.sum((temps - y_pred) ** 2)\n        ss_tot = np.sum((temps - np.mean(temps)) ** 2)\n        r_squared = 1 - (ss_res / ss_tot) if ss_tot > 0 else 0.0\n\n        # Calculate fitting error (RMSE)\n        fitting_error = np.sqrt(np.mean((temps - y_pred) ** 2))\n\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_est, 6),\n            \"tau\": round(tau_est, 4),\n            \"r_squared\": round(r_squared, 6),\n            \"fitting_error\": round(fitting_error, 6)\n        }\n\n        print(f\"Estimated K = {K_est:.6f} C/%\")\n        print(f\"Estimated tau = {tau_est:.4f} s\")\n        print(f\"R-squared = {r_squared:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"Fitting error (RMSE) = {fitting_error:.6f} C\")\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Parameter estimation failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Using fallback parameter estimates\")\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_guess, 6),\n            \"tau\": 30.0,\n            \"r_squared\": 0.0,\n            \"fitting_error\": 1.0\n        }\n\n    # Save estimated parameters\n    with open('/root/estimated_params.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(estimated_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    return estimated_params\n\n\ndef calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=0.5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate PI controller gains using IMC tuning rules.\n\n    IMC tuning for first-order system:\n    Kp = tau / (K * lambda)\n    Ki = 1 / lambda\n\n    Args:\n        estimated_params: Dictionary with K and tau\n        lambda_factor: IMC filter time constant multiplier\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with controller gains\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating controller gains using IMC tuning rules...\")\n\n    K = estimated_params[\"K\"]\n    tau = estimated_params[\"tau\"]\n\n    # IMC tuning parameter (desired closed-loop time constant)\n    # lambda should be chosen based on desired speed vs robustness\n    # Larger lambda = slower but more robust\n    # Using smaller lambda_factor for faster response to meet settling time requirement\n    lambda_c = lambda_factor * tau\n\n    # IMC tuning rules for first-order system\n    if K > 0:\n        Kp = tau / (K * lambda_c)\n        Ki = 1.0 / lambda_c\n    else:\n        print(\"Warning: Invalid K value, using default gains\")\n        Kp = 10.0\n        Ki = 0.1\n        lambda_c = 10.0\n\n    Kd = 0.0  # No derivative action for first-order system\n\n    tuned_gains = {\n        \"Kp\": round(Kp, 6),\n        \"Ki\": round(Ki, 6),\n        \"Kd\": round(Kd, 6),\n        \"lambda\": round(lambda_c, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Kp = {Kp:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Ki = {Ki:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Kd = {Kd:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Lambda (filter time constant) = {lambda_c:.4f} s\")\n\n    # Save tuned gains\n    with open('/root/tuned_gains.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(tuned_gains, f, indent=2)\n\n    return tuned_gains\n\n\ndef run_closed_loop_control(sim, tuned_gains, setpoint=22.0, duration=150.0, calibration_end_time=0.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run closed-loop temperature control.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance (already at calibration end state)\n        tuned_gains: Dictionary with Kp, Ki, Kd\n        setpoint: Target temperature (C)\n        duration: Control duration (seconds)\n        calibration_end_time: Time offset from calibration phase\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with control log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nRunning closed-loop control to {setpoint}C for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    controller = TemperatureController(\n        Kp=tuned_gains[\"Kp\"],\n        Ki=tuned_gains[\"Ki\"],\n        Kd=tuned_gains[\"Kd\"]\n    )\n\n    dt = sim.get_dt()\n    data = []\n\n    # Run control loop\n    steps = int(duration / dt)\n    for i in range(steps):\n        # Get current temperature (from last step or initial)\n        if i == 0:\n            # We're continuing from calibration, so step once to get current state\n            result = sim.step(0.0)\n        else:\n            # Compute control action\n            error = setpoint - current_temp\n            heater_power = controller.compute(error, dt)\n\n            # Apply control action\n            result = sim.step(heater_power)\n\n        current_temp = result[\"temperature\"]\n        current_time = calibration_end_time + result[\"time\"]\n        error = setpoint - current_temp\n\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": round(current_time, 2),\n            \"temperature\": round(current_temp, 4),\n            \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n            \"heater_power\": round(result[\"heater_power\"], 4),\n            \"error\": round(error, 4)\n        })\n\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    # Save control log\n    with open('/root/control_log.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Control complete: {len(data)} data points\")\n    final_temp = data[-1][\"temperature\"]\n    final_error = abs(final_temp - setpoint)\n    print(f\"Final temperature: {final_temp:.4f}C (error: {final_error:.4f}C)\")\n\n    return control_log\n\n\ndef calculate_metrics(control_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate performance metrics from control log.\n\n    Args:\n        control_log: Dictionary with control data\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating performance metrics...\")\n\n    data = control_log[\"data\"]\n    setpoint = control_log[\"setpoint\"]\n\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n    errors = np.array([d[\"error\"] for d in data])\n\n    # Initial temperature\n    T0 = temps[0]\n\n    # Rise time: time to go from 10% to 90% of setpoint change\n    delta_T = setpoint - T0\n    T_10 = T0 + 0.1 * delta_T\n    T_90 = T0 + 0.9 * delta_T\n\n    idx_10 = np.where(temps >= T_10)[0]\n    idx_90 = np.where(temps >= T_90)[0]\n\n    if len(idx_10) > 0 and len(idx_90) > 0:\n        rise_time = times[idx_90[0]] - times[idx_10[0]]\n    else:\n        rise_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Overshoot: maximum excursion beyond setpoint\n    max_temp = np.max(temps)\n    if max_temp > setpoint:\n        overshoot = (max_temp - setpoint) / abs(delta_T) if abs(delta_T) > 0 else 0.0\n    else:\n        overshoot = 0.0\n\n    # Settling time: time to stay within 2% of setpoint\n    settling_band = 0.02 * abs(delta_T)\n    settled = np.abs(errors) <= settling_band\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        # Find first time it enters and stays in band\n        first_settled_idx = np.where(settled)[0][0]\n        # Check if it stays settled\n        if np.all(settled[first_settled_idx:]):\n            settling_time = times[first_settled_idx] - times[0]\n        else:\n            # Find last time it enters the band permanently\n            for i in range(len(settled) - 1, -1, -1):\n                if np.all(settled[i:]):\n                    settling_time = times[i] - times[0]\n                    break\n            else:\n                settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n    else:\n        settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Steady-state error: average error over last 10% of data\n    steady_state_window = int(len(temps) * 0.1)\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(np.abs(errors[-steady_state_window:]))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"rise_time\": round(rise_time, 4),\n        \"overshoot\": round(overshoot, 6),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 6),\n        \"max_temp\": round(max_temp, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Rise time: {rise_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Overshoot: {overshoot*100:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"Settling time: {settling_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Steady-state error: {steady_state_error:.4f} C\")\n    print(f\"Max temperature: {max_temp:.4f} C\")\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open('/root/metrics.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Check constraints\n    print(\"\\nConstraint Verification:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error < 0.5C: {steady_state_error < 0.5} ({steady_state_error:.4f}C)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time < 120s: {settling_time < 120} ({settling_time:.4f}s)\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot < 10%: {overshoot < 0.10} ({overshoot*100:.2f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature < 30C: {max_temp < 30.0} ({max_temp:.4f}C)\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"TEMPERATURE CONTROLLER SYSTEM\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Reset simulator to start fresh for control phase\n    initial_temp = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Initial temperature: {initial_temp:.4f}C\")\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    controller = TemperatureController(\n        Kp=tuned_gains[\"Kp\"],\n        Ki=tuned_gains[\"Ki\"],\n        Kd=tuned_gains[\"Kd\"]\n    )\n\n    dt = sim.get_dt()\n    data = []\n\n    # Run control loop\n    steps = int(duration / dt)\n    current_temp = initial_temp\n\n    for i in range(steps):\n        # Compute control action\n        error = setpoint - current_temp\n        heater_power = controller.compute(error, dt)\n\n        # Apply control action\n        result = sim.step(heater_power)\n\n        current_temp = result[\"temperature\"]\n        current_time = result[\"time\"]\n\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": round(current_time, 2),\n            \"temperature\": round(current_temp, 4),\n            \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n            \"heater_power\": round(result[\"heater_power\"], 4),\n            \"error\": round(error, 4)\n        })","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nTemperature Controller Implementation\nPerforms calibration, parameter estimation, tuning, and closed-loop control.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import curve_fit\nfrom hvac_simulator import HVACSimulator\n\n\nclass TemperatureController:\n    \"\"\"PI controller for temperature regulation.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, Kp, Ki, Kd=0.0):\n        \"\"\"Initialize controller with gains.\"\"\"\n        self.Kp = Kp\n        self.Ki = Ki\n        self.Kd = Kd\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n    def reset(self):\n        \"\"\"Reset controller state.\"\"\"\n        self.integral = 0.0\n        self.prev_error = 0.0\n\n    def compute(self, error, dt):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute control output using PI control.\n\n        Args:\n            error: Current error (setpoint - measurement)\n            dt: Time step\n\n        Returns:\n            Control output (heater power 0-100%)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Proportional term\n        P = self.Kp * error\n\n        # Integral term with anti-windup\n        self.integral += error * dt\n        I = self.Ki * self.integral\n\n        # Derivative term\n        D = 0.0\n        if dt > 0:\n            D = self.Kd * (error - self.prev_error) / dt\n        self.prev_error = error\n\n        # Compute output\n        output = P + I + D\n\n        # Clamp output to valid range\n        output = np.clip(output, 0.0, 100.0)\n\n        # Anti-windup: prevent integral windup\n        if output >= 100.0 or output <= 0.0:\n            self.integral -= error * dt\n\n        return output\n\n\ndef run_calibration(sim, heater_power_test=50.0, duration=35.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run step test for system identification.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        heater_power_test: Step input power (%)\n        duration: Test duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with calibration data\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running calibration test with {heater_power_test}% heater power for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Reset simulator\n    initial_temp = sim.reset()\n\n    # Collect initial data point\n    data = [{\n        \"time\": 0.0,\n        \"temperature\": round(initial_temp, 4),\n        \"heater_power\": 0.0\n    }]\n\n    # Run open-loop step test\n    results = sim.run_open_loop(heater_power_test, duration)\n\n    for result in results:\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": result[\"time\"],\n            \"temperature\": result[\"temperature\"],\n            \"heater_power\": result[\"heater_power\"]\n        })\n\n    calibration_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"calibration\",\n        \"heater_power_test\": heater_power_test,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    # Save calibration log\n    with open('/root/calibration_log.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(calibration_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Calibration complete: {len(data)} data points collected\")\n    print(f\"Temperature range: {min(d['temperature'] for d in data):.2f}C to {max(d['temperature'] for d in data):.2f}C\")\n\n    return calibration_log\n\n\ndef estimate_parameters(calibration_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Estimate first-order system parameters (K, tau) from step response.\n\n    Args:\n        calibration_log: Dictionary with calibration data\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with estimated parameters\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nEstimating system parameters from calibration data...\")\n\n    data = calibration_log[\"data\"]\n    u_step = calibration_log[\"heater_power_test\"]\n\n    # Extract time series\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n\n    # Initial temperature (average of first few points to reduce noise)\n    T0 = np.mean(temps[:min(5, len(temps))])\n\n    # Ambient temperature (assume initial temp is ambient)\n    T_ambient = T0\n\n    # First-order step response model:\n    # T(t) = T_ambient + K*u*(1 - exp(-t/tau))\n    def first_order_response(t, K, tau):\n        return T_ambient + K * u_step * (1 - np.exp(-t / tau))\n\n    # Initial parameter guesses\n    # K: roughly (final_temp - initial_temp) / u_step\n    # tau: time to reach 63.2% of final value\n    delta_T = temps[-1] - T0\n    K_guess = delta_T / u_step if u_step > 0 else 0.1\n    tau_guess = 30.0\n\n    try:\n        # Fit the model\n        params, covariance = curve_fit(\n            first_order_response,\n            times,\n            temps,\n            p0=[K_guess, tau_guess],\n            bounds=([0.01, 1.0], [1.0, 200.0]),\n            maxfev=10000\n        )\n\n        K_est, tau_est = params\n\n        # Calculate R-squared\n        y_pred = first_order_response(times, K_est, tau_est)\n        ss_res = np.sum((temps - y_pred) ** 2)\n        ss_tot = np.sum((temps - np.mean(temps)) ** 2)\n        r_squared = 1 - (ss_res / ss_tot) if ss_tot > 0 else 0.0\n\n        # Calculate fitting error (RMSE)\n        fitting_error = np.sqrt(np.mean((temps - y_pred) ** 2))\n\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_est, 6),\n            \"tau\": round(tau_est, 4),\n            \"r_squared\": round(r_squared, 6),\n            \"fitting_error\": round(fitting_error, 6)\n        }\n\n        print(f\"Estimated K = {K_est:.6f} C/%\")\n        print(f\"Estimated tau = {tau_est:.4f} s\")\n        print(f\"R-squared = {r_squared:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"Fitting error (RMSE) = {fitting_error:.6f} C\")\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Parameter estimation failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Using fallback parameter estimates\")\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_guess, 6),\n            \"tau\": 30.0,\n            \"r_squared\": 0.0,\n            \"fitting_error\": 1.0\n        }\n\n    # Save estimated parameters\n    with open('/root/estimated_params.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(estimated_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    return estimated_params\n\n\ndef calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=0.5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate PI controller gains using IMC tuning rules.\n\n    IMC tuning for first-order system:\n    Kp = tau / (K * lambda)\n    Ki = 1 / lambda\n\n    Args:\n        estimated_params: Dictionary with K and tau\n        lambda_factor: IMC filter time constant multiplier\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with controller gains\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating controller gains using IMC tuning rules...\")\n\n    K = estimated_params[\"K\"]\n    tau = estimated_params[\"tau\"]\n\n    # IMC tuning parameter (desired closed-loop time constant)\n    # lambda should be chosen based on desired speed vs robustness\n    # Larger lambda = slower but more robust\n    # Using smaller lambda_factor for faster response to meet settling time requirement\n    lambda_c = lambda_factor * tau\n\n    # IMC tuning rules for first-order system\n    if K > 0:\n        Kp = tau / (K * lambda_c)\n        Ki = 1.0 / lambda_c\n    else:\n        print(\"Warning: Invalid K value, using default gains\")\n        Kp = 10.0\n        Ki = 0.1\n        lambda_c = 10.0\n\n    Kd = 0.0  # No derivative action for first-order system\n\n    tuned_gains = {\n        \"Kp\": round(Kp, 6),\n        \"Ki\": round(Ki, 6),\n        \"Kd\": round(Kd, 6),\n        \"lambda\": round(lambda_c, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Kp = {Kp:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Ki = {Ki:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Kd = {Kd:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Lambda (filter time constant) = {lambda_c:.4f} s\")\n\n    # Save tuned gains\n    with open('/root/tuned_gains.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(tuned_gains, f, indent=2)\n\n    return tuned_gains\n\n\ndef run_closed_loop_control(sim, tuned_gains, setpoint=22.0, duration=150.0, calibration_end_time=0.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run closed-loop temperature control.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance (already at calibration end state)\n        tuned_gains: Dictionary with Kp, Ki, Kd\n        setpoint: Target temperature (C)\n        duration: Control duration (seconds)\n        calibration_end_time: Time offset from calibration phase\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with control log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nRunning closed-loop control to {setpoint}C for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    controller = TemperatureController(\n        Kp=tuned_gains[\"Kp\"],\n        Ki=tuned_gains[\"Ki\"],\n        Kd=tuned_gains[\"Kd\"]\n    )\n\n    dt = sim.get_dt()\n    data = []\n\n    # Run control loop\n    steps = int(duration / dt)\n    for i in range(steps):\n        # Get current temperature (from last step or initial)\n        if i == 0:\n            # We're continuing from calibration, so step once to get current state\n            result = sim.step(0.0)\n        else:\n            # Compute control action\n            error = setpoint - current_temp\n            heater_power = controller.compute(error, dt)\n\n            # Apply control action\n            result = sim.step(heater_power)\n\n        current_temp = result[\"temperature\"]\n        current_time = calibration_end_time + result[\"time\"]\n        error = setpoint - current_temp\n\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": round(current_time, 2),\n            \"temperature\": round(current_temp, 4),\n            \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n            \"heater_power\": round(result[\"heater_power\"], 4),\n            \"error\": round(error, 4)\n        })\n\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    # Save control log\n    with open('/root/control_log.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Control complete: {len(data)} data points\")\n    final_temp = data[-1][\"temperature\"]\n    final_error = abs(final_temp - setpoint)\n    print(f\"Final temperature: {final_temp:.4f}C (error: {final_error:.4f}C)\")\n\n    return control_log\n\n\ndef calculate_metrics(control_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate performance metrics from control log.\n\n    Args:\n        control_log: Dictionary with control data\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating performance metrics...\")\n\n    data = control_log[\"data\"]\n    setpoint = control_log[\"setpoint\"]\n\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n    errors = np.array([d[\"error\"] for d in data])\n\n    # Initial temperature\n    T0 = temps[0]\n\n    # Rise time: time to go from 10% to 90% of setpoint change\n    delta_T = setpoint - T0\n    T_10 = T0 + 0.1 * delta_T\n    T_90 = T0 + 0.9 * delta_T\n\n    idx_10 = np.where(temps >= T_10)[0]\n    idx_90 = np.where(temps >= T_90)[0]\n\n    if len(idx_10) > 0 and len(idx_90) > 0:\n        rise_time = times[idx_90[0]] - times[idx_10[0]]\n    else:\n        rise_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Overshoot: maximum excursion beyond setpoint\n    max_temp = np.max(temps)\n    if max_temp > setpoint:\n        overshoot = (max_temp - setpoint) / abs(delta_T) if abs(delta_T) > 0 else 0.0\n    else:\n        overshoot = 0.0\n\n    # Settling time: time to stay within 2% of setpoint\n    settling_band = 0.02 * abs(delta_T)\n    settled = np.abs(errors) <= settling_band\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        # Find first time it enters and stays in band\n        first_settled_idx = np.where(settled)[0][0]\n        # Check if it stays settled\n        if np.all(settled[first_settled_idx:]):\n            settling_time = times[first_settled_idx] - times[0]\n        else:\n            # Find last time it enters the band permanently\n            for i in range(len(settled) - 1, -1, -1):\n                if np.all(settled[i:]):\n                    settling_time = times[i] - times[0]\n                    break\n            else:\n                settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n    else:\n        settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Steady-state error: average error over last 10% of data\n    steady_state_window = int(len(temps) * 0.1)\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(np.abs(errors[-steady_state_window:]))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"rise_time\": round(rise_time, 4),\n        \"overshoot\": round(overshoot, 6),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 6),\n        \"max_temp\": round(max_temp, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Rise time: {rise_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Overshoot: {overshoot*100:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"Settling time: {settling_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Steady-state error: {steady_state_error:.4f} C\")\n    print(f\"Max temperature: {max_temp:.4f} C\")\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open('/root/metrics.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Check constraints\n    print(\"\\nConstraint Verification:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error < 0.5C: {steady_state_error < 0.5} ({steady_state_error:.4f}C)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time < 120s: {settling_time < 120} ({settling_time:.4f}s)\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot < 10%: {overshoot < 0.10} ({overshoot*100:.2f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature < 30C: {max_temp < 30.0} ({max_temp:.4f}C)\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"TEMPERATURE CONTROLLER SYSTEM\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Initialize simulator\n    sim = HVACSimulator()\n    print(f\"\\nSimulator initialized:\")\n    print(f\"  Setpoint: {sim.get_setpoint()}C\")\n    print(f\"  Ambient: {sim.get_ambient_temp()}C\")\n    print(f\"  Safety limits: {sim.get_safety_limits()}\")\n    print(f\"  Timestep: {sim.get_dt()}s\")\n\n    # Proportional term\n        P = self.Kp * error\n\n        # Integral term with anti-windup\n        self.integral += error * dt\n        I = self.Ki * self.integral\n\n        # Derivative term\n        D = 0.0\n        if dt > 0:\n            D = self.Kd * (error - self.prev_error) / dt\n        self.prev_error = error\n\n        # Compute output\n        output = P + I + D\n\n        # Clamp output to valid range\n        output = np.clip(output, 0.0, 100.0)\n\n        # Anti-windup: prevent integral windup\n        if output >= 100.0 or output <= 0.0:\n            self.integral -= error * dt\n\n        return output\n\n\ndef run_calibration(sim, heater_power_test=50.0, duration=35.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run step test for system identification.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        heater_power_test: Step input power (%)\n        duration: Test duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with calibration data\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running calibration test with {heater_power_test}% heater power for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Extract time series\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n\n    # Initial temperature (average of first few points to reduce noise)\n    T0 = np.mean(temps[:min(5, len(temps))])\n\n    # Ambient temperature (assume initial temp is ambient)\n    T_ambient = T0\n\n    # First-order step response model:\n    # T(t) = T_ambient + K*u*(1 - exp(-t/tau))\n    def first_order_response(t, K, tau):\n        return T_ambient + K * u_step * (1 - np.exp(-t / tau))\n\n    # Initial parameter guesses\n    # K: roughly (final_temp - initial_temp) / u_step\n    # tau: time to reach 63.2% of final value\n    delta_T = temps[-1] - T0\n    K_guess = delta_T / u_step if u_step > 0 else 0.1\n    tau_guess = 30.0\n\n    try:\n        # Fit the model\n        params, covariance = curve_fit(\n            first_order_response,\n            times,\n            temps,\n            p0=[K_guess, tau_guess],\n            bounds=([0.01, 1.0], [1.0, 200.0]),\n            maxfev=10000\n        )\n\n        K_est, tau_est = params\n\n        # Calculate R-squared\n        y_pred = first_order_response(times, K_est, tau_est)\n        ss_res = np.sum((temps - y_pred) ** 2)\n        ss_tot = np.sum((temps - np.mean(temps)) ** 2)\n        r_squared = 1 - (ss_res / ss_tot) if ss_tot > 0 else 0.0\n\n        # Calculate fitting error (RMSE)\n        fitting_error = np.sqrt(np.mean((temps - y_pred) ** 2))\n\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_est, 6),\n            \"tau\": round(tau_est, 4),\n            \"r_squared\": round(r_squared, 6),\n            \"fitting_error\": round(fitting_error, 6)\n        }\n\n        print(f\"Estimated K = {K_est:.6f} C/%\")\n        print(f\"Estimated tau = {tau_est:.4f} s\")\n        print(f\"R-squared = {r_squared:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"Fitting error (RMSE) = {fitting_error:.6f} C\")\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Parameter estimation failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Using fallback parameter estimates\")\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_guess, 6),\n            \"tau\": 30.0,\n            \"r_squared\": 0.0,\n            \"fitting_error\": 1.0\n        }\n\n    # Save estimated parameters\n    with open('/root/estimated_params.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(estimated_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    return estimated_params\n\n\ndef calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=0.5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate PI controller gains using IMC tuning rules.\n\n    IMC tuning for first-order system:\n    Kp = tau / (K * lambda)\n    Ki = 1 / lambda\n\n    Args:\n        estimated_params: Dictionary with K and tau\n        lambda_factor: IMC filter time constant multiplier\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with controller gains\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating controller gains using IMC tuning rules...\")\n\n    K = estimated_params[\"K\"]\n    tau = estimated_params[\"tau\"]\n\n    # IMC tuning parameter (desired closed-loop time constant)\n    # lambda should be chosen based on desired speed vs robustness\n    # Larger lambda = slower but more robust\n    # Using smaller lambda_factor for faster response to meet settling time requirement\n    lambda_c = lambda_factor * tau\n\n    # IMC tuning rules for first-order system\n    if K > 0:\n        Kp = tau / (K * lambda_c)\n        Ki = 1.0 / lambda_c\n    else:\n        print(\"Warning: Invalid K value, using default gains\")\n        Kp = 10.0\n        Ki = 0.1\n        lambda_c = 10.0\n\n    Kd = 0.0  # No derivative action for first-order system\n\n    tuned_gains = {\n        \"Kp\": round(Kp, 6),\n        \"Ki\": round(Ki, 6),\n        \"Kd\": round(Kd, 6),\n        \"lambda\": round(lambda_c, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Kp = {Kp:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Ki = {Ki:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Kd = {Kd:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Lambda (filter time constant) = {lambda_c:.4f} s\")\n\n    # Initial temperature\n    T0 = temps[0]\n\n    # Rise time: time to go from 10% to 90% of setpoint change\n    delta_T = setpoint - T0\n    T_10 = T0 + 0.1 * delta_T\n    T_90 = T0 + 0.9 * delta_T\n\n    idx_10 = np.where(temps >= T_10)[0]\n    idx_90 = np.where(temps >= T_90)[0]\n\n    if len(idx_10) > 0 and len(idx_90) > 0:\n        rise_time = times[idx_90[0]] - times[idx_10[0]]\n    else:\n        rise_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Overshoot: maximum excursion beyond setpoint\n    max_temp = np.max(temps)\n    if max_temp > setpoint:\n        overshoot = (max_temp - setpoint) / abs(delta_T) if abs(delta_T) > 0 else 0.0\n    else:\n        overshoot = 0.0\n\n    # Settling time: time to stay within 2% of setpoint\n    settling_band = 0.02 * abs(delta_T)\n    settled = np.abs(errors) <= settling_band\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        # Find first time it enters and stays in band\n        first_settled_idx = np.where(settled)[0][0]\n        # Check if it stays settled\n        if np.all(settled[first_settled_idx:]):\n            settling_time = times[first_settled_idx] - times[0]\n        else:\n            # Find last time it enters the band permanently\n            for i in range(len(settled) - 1, -1, -1):\n                if np.all(settled[i:]):\n                    settling_time = times[i] - times[0]\n                    break\n            else:\n                settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n    else:\n        settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Steady-state error: average error over last 10% of data\n    steady_state_window = int(len(temps) * 0.1)\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(np.abs(errors[-steady_state_window:]))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"rise_time\": round(rise_time, 4),\n        \"overshoot\": round(overshoot, 6),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 6),\n        \"max_temp\": round(max_temp, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Rise time: {rise_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Overshoot: {overshoot*100:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"Settling time: {settling_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Steady-state error: {steady_state_error:.4f} C\")\n    print(f\"Max temperature: {max_temp:.4f} C\")\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open('/root/metrics.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Check constraints\n    print(\"\\nConstraint Verification:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error < 0.5C: {steady_state_error < 0.5} ({steady_state_error:.4f}C)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time < 120s: {settling_time < 120} ({settling_time:.4f}s)\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot < 10%: {overshoot < 0.10} ({overshoot*100:.2f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature < 30C: {max_temp < 30.0} ({max_temp:.4f}C)\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"TEMPERATURE CONTROLLER SYSTEM\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Proportional term\n        P = self.Kp * error\n\n        # Integral term with anti-windup\n        self.integral += error * dt\n        I = self.Ki * self.integral\n\n        # Derivative term\n        D = 0.0\n        if dt > 0:\n            D = self.Kd * (error - self.prev_error) / dt\n        self.prev_error = error\n\n        # Compute output\n        output = P + I + D\n\n        # Clamp output to valid range\n        output = np.clip(output, 0.0, 100.0)\n\n        # Anti-windup: prevent integral windup\n        if output >= 100.0 or output <= 0.0:\n            self.integral -= error * dt\n\n        return output\n\n\ndef run_calibration(sim, heater_power_test=50.0, duration=35.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run step test for system identification.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance\n        heater_power_test: Step input power (%)\n        duration: Test duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with calibration data\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Running calibration test with {heater_power_test}% heater power for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Extract time series\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n\n    # Initial temperature (average of first few points to reduce noise)\n    T0 = np.mean(temps[:min(5, len(temps))])\n\n    # Ambient temperature (assume initial temp is ambient)\n    T_ambient = T0\n\n    # First-order step response model:\n    # T(t) = T_ambient + K*u*(1 - exp(-t/tau))\n    def first_order_response(t, K, tau):\n        return T_ambient + K * u_step * (1 - np.exp(-t / tau))\n\n    # Initial parameter guesses\n    # K: roughly (final_temp - initial_temp) / u_step\n    # tau: time to reach 63.2% of final value\n    delta_T = temps[-1] - T0\n    K_guess = delta_T / u_step if u_step > 0 else 0.1\n    tau_guess = 30.0\n\n    try:\n        # Fit the model\n        params, covariance = curve_fit(\n            first_order_response,\n            times,\n            temps,\n            p0=[K_guess, tau_guess],\n            bounds=([0.01, 1.0], [1.0, 200.0]),\n            maxfev=10000\n        )\n\n        K_est, tau_est = params\n\n        # Calculate R-squared\n        y_pred = first_order_response(times, K_est, tau_est)\n        ss_res = np.sum((temps - y_pred) ** 2)\n        ss_tot = np.sum((temps - np.mean(temps)) ** 2)\n        r_squared = 1 - (ss_res / ss_tot) if ss_tot > 0 else 0.0\n\n        # Calculate fitting error (RMSE)\n        fitting_error = np.sqrt(np.mean((temps - y_pred) ** 2))\n\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_est, 6),\n            \"tau\": round(tau_est, 4),\n            \"r_squared\": round(r_squared, 6),\n            \"fitting_error\": round(fitting_error, 6)\n        }\n\n        print(f\"Estimated K = {K_est:.6f} C/%\")\n        print(f\"Estimated tau = {tau_est:.4f} s\")\n        print(f\"R-squared = {r_squared:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"Fitting error (RMSE) = {fitting_error:.6f} C\")\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Parameter estimation failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Using fallback parameter estimates\")\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_guess, 6),\n            \"tau\": 30.0,\n            \"r_squared\": 0.0,\n            \"fitting_error\": 1.0\n        }\n\n    # Save estimated parameters\n    with open('/root/estimated_params.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(estimated_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    return estimated_params\n\n\ndef calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=0.5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate PI controller gains using IMC tuning rules.\n\n    IMC tuning for first-order system:\n    Kp = tau / (K * lambda)\n    Ki = 1 / lambda\n\n    Args:\n        estimated_params: Dictionary with K and tau\n        lambda_factor: IMC filter time constant multiplier\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with controller gains\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating controller gains using IMC tuning rules...\")\n\n    K = estimated_params[\"K\"]\n    tau = estimated_params[\"tau\"]\n\n    # IMC tuning parameter (desired closed-loop time constant)\n    # lambda should be chosen based on desired speed vs robustness\n    # Larger lambda = slower but more robust\n    # Using smaller lambda_factor for faster response to meet settling time requirement\n    lambda_c = lambda_factor * tau\n\n    # IMC tuning rules for first-order system\n    if K > 0:\n        Kp = tau / (K * lambda_c)\n        Ki = 1.0 / lambda_c\n    else:\n        print(\"Warning: Invalid K value, using default gains\")\n        Kp = 10.0\n        Ki = 0.1\n        lambda_c = 10.0\n\n    Kd = 0.0  # No derivative action for first-order system\n\n    tuned_gains = {\n        \"Kp\": round(Kp, 6),\n        \"Ki\": round(Ki, 6),\n        \"Kd\": round(Kd, 6),\n        \"lambda\": round(lambda_c, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Kp = {Kp:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Ki = {Ki:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Kd = {Kd:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Lambda (filter time constant) = {lambda_c:.4f} s\")\n\n    # Save tuned gains\n    with open('/root/tuned_gains.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(tuned_gains, f, indent=2)\n\n    return tuned_gains\n\n\ndef run_closed_loop_control(sim, tuned_gains, setpoint=22.0, duration=150.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run closed-loop temperature control.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance (will be reset)\n        tuned_gains: Dictionary with Kp, Ki, Kd\n        setpoint: Target temperature (C)\n        duration: Control duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with control log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nRunning closed-loop control to {setpoint}C for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Apply control action\n        result = sim.step(heater_power)\n\n        current_temp = result[\"temperature\"]\n        current_time = result[\"time\"]\n\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": round(current_time, 2),\n            \"temperature\": round(current_temp, 4),\n            \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n            \"heater_power\": round(result[\"heater_power\"], 4),\n            \"error\": round(error, 4)\n        })\n\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    # Initial temperature\n    T0 = temps[0]\n\n    # Rise time: time to go from 10% to 90% of setpoint change\n    delta_T = setpoint - T0\n    T_10 = T0 + 0.1 * delta_T\n    T_90 = T0 + 0.9 * delta_T\n\n    idx_10 = np.where(temps >= T_10)[0]\n    idx_90 = np.where(temps >= T_90)[0]\n\n    if len(idx_10) > 0 and len(idx_90) > 0:\n        rise_time = times[idx_90[0]] - times[idx_10[0]]\n    else:\n        rise_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Overshoot: maximum excursion beyond setpoint\n    max_temp = np.max(temps)\n    if max_temp > setpoint:\n        overshoot = (max_temp - setpoint) / abs(delta_T) if abs(delta_T) > 0 else 0.0\n    else:\n        overshoot = 0.0\n\n    # Settling time: time to stay within 2% of setpoint\n    settling_band = 0.02 * abs(delta_T)\n    settled = np.abs(errors) <= settling_band\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        # Find first time it enters and stays in band\n        first_settled_idx = np.where(settled)[0][0]\n        # Check if it stays settled\n        if np.all(settled[first_settled_idx:]):\n            settling_time = times[first_settled_idx] - times[0]\n        else:\n            # Find last time it enters the band permanently\n            for i in range(len(settled) - 1, -1, -1):\n                if np.all(settled[i:]):\n                    settling_time = times[i] - times[0]\n                    break\n            else:\n                settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n    else:\n        settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Steady-state error: average error over last 10% of data\n    steady_state_window = int(len(temps) * 0.1)\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(np.abs(errors[-steady_state_window:]))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"rise_time\": round(rise_time, 4),\n        \"overshoot\": round(overshoot, 6),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 6),\n        \"max_temp\": round(max_temp, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Rise time: {rise_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Overshoot: {overshoot*100:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"Settling time: {settling_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Steady-state error: {steady_state_error:.4f} C\")\n    print(f\"Max temperature: {max_temp:.4f} C\")\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open('/root/metrics.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Check constraints\n    print(\"\\nConstraint Verification:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error < 0.5C: {steady_state_error < 0.5} ({steady_state_error:.4f}C)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time < 120s: {settling_time < 120} ({settling_time:.4f}s)\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot < 10%: {overshoot < 0.10} ({overshoot*100:.2f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature < 30C: {max_temp < 30.0} ({max_temp:.4f}C)\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"TEMPERATURE CONTROLLER SYSTEM\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Extract time series\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n\n    # Initial temperature (average of first few points to reduce noise)\n    T0 = np.mean(temps[:min(5, len(temps))])\n\n    # Ambient temperature (assume initial temp is ambient)\n    T_ambient = T0\n\n    # First-order step response model:\n    # T(t) = T_ambient + K*u*(1 - exp(-t/tau))\n    def first_order_response(t, K, tau):\n        return T_ambient + K * u_step * (1 - np.exp(-t / tau))\n\n    # Initial parameter guesses\n    # K: roughly (final_temp - initial_temp) / u_step\n    # tau: time to reach 63.2% of final value\n    delta_T = temps[-1] - T0\n    K_guess = delta_T / u_step if u_step > 0 else 0.1\n    tau_guess = 30.0\n\n    try:\n        # Fit the model\n        params, covariance = curve_fit(\n            first_order_response,\n            times,\n            temps,\n            p0=[K_guess, tau_guess],\n            bounds=([0.01, 1.0], [1.0, 200.0]),\n            maxfev=10000\n        )\n\n        K_est, tau_est = params\n\n        # Calculate R-squared\n        y_pred = first_order_response(times, K_est, tau_est)\n        ss_res = np.sum((temps - y_pred) ** 2)\n        ss_tot = np.sum((temps - np.mean(temps)) ** 2)\n        r_squared = 1 - (ss_res / ss_tot) if ss_tot > 0 else 0.0\n\n        # Calculate fitting error (RMSE)\n        fitting_error = np.sqrt(np.mean((temps - y_pred) ** 2))\n\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_est, 6),\n            \"tau\": round(tau_est, 4),\n            \"r_squared\": round(r_squared, 6),\n            \"fitting_error\": round(fitting_error, 6)\n        }\n\n        print(f\"Estimated K = {K_est:.6f} C/%\")\n        print(f\"Estimated tau = {tau_est:.4f} s\")\n        print(f\"R-squared = {r_squared:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"Fitting error (RMSE) = {fitting_error:.6f} C\")\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Parameter estimation failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Using fallback parameter estimates\")\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_guess, 6),\n            \"tau\": 30.0,\n            \"r_squared\": 0.0,\n            \"fitting_error\": 1.0\n        }\n\n    # Save estimated parameters\n    with open('/root/estimated_params.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(estimated_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    return estimated_params\n\n\ndef calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=0.3):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate PI controller gains using IMC tuning rules.\n\n    IMC tuning for first-order system:\n    Kp = tau / (K * lambda)\n    Ki = 1 / lambda\n\n    Args:\n        estimated_params: Dictionary with K and tau\n        lambda_factor: IMC filter time constant multiplier\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with controller gains\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating controller gains using IMC tuning rules...\")\n\n    K = estimated_params[\"K\"]\n    tau = estimated_params[\"tau\"]\n\n    # IMC tuning parameter (desired closed-loop time constant)\n    # lambda should be chosen based on desired speed vs robustness\n    # Larger lambda = slower but more robust\n    # Using smaller lambda_factor for faster response to meet settling time requirement\n    lambda_c = lambda_factor * tau\n\n    # IMC tuning rules for first-order system\n    if K > 0:\n        Kp = tau / (K * lambda_c)\n        Ki = 1.0 / lambda_c\n    else:\n        print(\"Warning: Invalid K value, using default gains\")\n        Kp = 10.0\n        Ki = 0.1\n        lambda_c = 10.0\n\n    Kd = 0.0  # No derivative action for first-order system\n\n    tuned_gains = {\n        \"Kp\": round(Kp, 6),\n        \"Ki\": round(Ki, 6),\n        \"Kd\": round(Kd, 6),\n        \"lambda\": round(lambda_c, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Kp = {Kp:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Ki = {Ki:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Kd = {Kd:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Lambda (filter time constant) = {lambda_c:.4f} s\")\n\n    # Save tuned gains\n    with open('/root/tuned_gains.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(tuned_gains, f, indent=2)\n\n    return tuned_gains\n\n\ndef run_closed_loop_control(sim, tuned_gains, setpoint=22.0, duration=150.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run closed-loop temperature control.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance (will be reset)\n        tuned_gains: Dictionary with Kp, Ki, Kd\n        setpoint: Target temperature (C)\n        duration: Control duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with control log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nRunning closed-loop control to {setpoint}C for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Reset simulator to start fresh for control phase\n    initial_temp = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Initial temperature: {initial_temp:.4f}C\")\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    controller = TemperatureController(\n        Kp=tuned_gains[\"Kp\"],\n        Ki=tuned_gains[\"Ki\"],\n        Kd=tuned_gains[\"Kd\"]\n    )\n\n    dt = sim.get_dt()\n    data = []\n\n    # Run control loop\n    steps = int(duration / dt)\n    current_temp = initial_temp\n\n    for i in range(steps):\n        # Compute control action\n        error = setpoint - current_temp\n        heater_power = controller.compute(error, dt)\n\n        # Apply control action\n        result = sim.step(heater_power)\n\n        current_temp = result[\"temperature\"]\n        current_time = result[\"time\"]\n\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": round(current_time, 2),\n            \"temperature\": round(current_temp, 4),\n            \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n            \"heater_power\": round(result[\"heater_power\"], 4),\n            \"error\": round(error, 4)\n        })\n\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    # Initial temperature\n    T0 = temps[0]\n\n    # Rise time: time to go from 10% to 90% of setpoint change\n    delta_T = setpoint - T0\n    T_10 = T0 + 0.1 * delta_T\n    T_90 = T0 + 0.9 * delta_T\n\n    idx_10 = np.where(temps >= T_10)[0]\n    idx_90 = np.where(temps >= T_90)[0]\n\n    if len(idx_10) > 0 and len(idx_90) > 0:\n        rise_time = times[idx_90[0]] - times[idx_10[0]]\n    else:\n        rise_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Overshoot: maximum excursion beyond setpoint\n    max_temp = np.max(temps)\n    if max_temp > setpoint:\n        overshoot = (max_temp - setpoint) / abs(delta_T) if abs(delta_T) > 0 else 0.0\n    else:\n        overshoot = 0.0\n\n    # Settling time: time to stay within 2% of setpoint\n    settling_band = 0.02 * abs(delta_T)\n    settled = np.abs(errors) <= settling_band\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        # Find first time it enters and stays in band\n        first_settled_idx = np.where(settled)[0][0]\n        # Check if it stays settled\n        if np.all(settled[first_settled_idx:]):\n            settling_time = times[first_settled_idx] - times[0]\n        else:\n            # Find last time it enters the band permanently\n            for i in range(len(settled) - 1, -1, -1):\n                if np.all(settled[i:]):\n                    settling_time = times[i] - times[0]\n                    break\n            else:\n                settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n    else:\n        settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Steady-state error: average error over last 10% of data\n    steady_state_window = int(len(temps) * 0.1)\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(np.abs(errors[-steady_state_window:]))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"rise_time\": round(rise_time, 4),\n        \"overshoot\": round(overshoot, 6),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 6),\n        \"max_temp\": round(max_temp, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Rise time: {rise_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Overshoot: {overshoot*100:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"Settling time: {settling_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Steady-state error: {steady_state_error:.4f} C\")\n    print(f\"Max temperature: {max_temp:.4f} C\")\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open('/root/metrics.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Check constraints\n    print(\"\\nConstraint Verification:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error < 0.5C: {steady_state_error < 0.5} ({steady_state_error:.4f}C)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time < 120s: {settling_time < 120} ({settling_time:.4f}s)\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot < 10%: {overshoot < 0.10} ({overshoot*100:.2f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature < 30C: {max_temp < 30.0} ({max_temp:.4f}C)\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"TEMPERATURE CONTROLLER SYSTEM\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Run open-loop step test\n    results = sim.run_open_loop(heater_power_test, duration)\n\n    for result in results:\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": result[\"time\"],\n            \"temperature\": result[\"temperature\"],\n            \"heater_power\": result[\"heater_power\"]\n        })\n\n    calibration_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"calibration\",\n        \"heater_power_test\": heater_power_test,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    # Extract time series\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n\n    # Initial temperature (average of first few points to reduce noise)\n    T0 = np.mean(temps[:min(5, len(temps))])\n\n    # Ambient temperature (assume initial temp is ambient)\n    T_ambient = T0\n\n    # First-order step response model:\n    # T(t) = T_ambient + K*u*(1 - exp(-t/tau))\n    def first_order_response(t, K, tau):\n        return T_ambient + K * u_step * (1 - np.exp(-t / tau))\n\n    # Initial parameter guesses\n    # K: roughly (final_temp - initial_temp) / u_step\n    # tau: time to reach 63.2% of final value\n    delta_T = temps[-1] - T0\n    K_guess = delta_T / u_step if u_step > 0 else 0.1\n    tau_guess = 30.0\n\n    try:\n        # Fit the model\n        params, covariance = curve_fit(\n            first_order_response,\n            times,\n            temps,\n            p0=[K_guess, tau_guess],\n            bounds=([0.01, 1.0], [1.0, 200.0]),\n            maxfev=10000\n        )\n\n        K_est, tau_est = params\n\n        # Calculate R-squared\n        y_pred = first_order_response(times, K_est, tau_est)\n        ss_res = np.sum((temps - y_pred) ** 2)\n        ss_tot = np.sum((temps - np.mean(temps)) ** 2)\n        r_squared = 1 - (ss_res / ss_tot) if ss_tot > 0 else 0.0\n\n        # Calculate fitting error (RMSE)\n        fitting_error = np.sqrt(np.mean((temps - y_pred) ** 2))\n\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_est, 6),\n            \"tau\": round(tau_est, 4),\n            \"r_squared\": round(r_squared, 6),\n            \"fitting_error\": round(fitting_error, 6)\n        }\n\n        print(f\"Estimated K = {K_est:.6f} C/%\")\n        print(f\"Estimated tau = {tau_est:.4f} s\")\n        print(f\"R-squared = {r_squared:.6f}\")\n        print(f\"Fitting error (RMSE) = {fitting_error:.6f} C\")\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Parameter estimation failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Using fallback parameter estimates\")\n        estimated_params = {\n            \"K\": round(K_guess, 6),\n            \"tau\": 30.0,\n            \"r_squared\": 0.0,\n            \"fitting_error\": 1.0\n        }\n\n    # Save estimated parameters\n    with open('/root/estimated_params.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(estimated_params, f, indent=2)\n\n    return estimated_params\n\n\ndef calculate_controller_gains(estimated_params, lambda_factor=0.3):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate PI controller gains using IMC tuning rules.\n\n    IMC tuning for first-order system:\n    Kp = tau / (K * lambda)\n    Ki = 1 / lambda\n\n    Args:\n        estimated_params: Dictionary with K and tau\n        lambda_factor: IMC filter time constant multiplier\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with controller gains\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating controller gains using IMC tuning rules...\")\n\n    K = estimated_params[\"K\"]\n    tau = estimated_params[\"tau\"]\n\n    # IMC tuning parameter (desired closed-loop time constant)\n    # lambda should be chosen based on desired speed vs robustness\n    # Larger lambda = slower but more robust\n    # Using smaller lambda_factor for faster response to meet settling time requirement\n    lambda_c = lambda_factor * tau\n\n    # IMC tuning rules for first-order system\n    if K > 0:\n        Kp = tau / (K * lambda_c)\n        Ki = 1.0 / lambda_c\n    else:\n        print(\"Warning: Invalid K value, using default gains\")\n        Kp = 10.0\n        Ki = 0.1\n        lambda_c = 10.0\n\n    Kd = 0.0  # No derivative action for first-order system\n\n    tuned_gains = {\n        \"Kp\": round(Kp, 6),\n        \"Ki\": round(Ki, 6),\n        \"Kd\": round(Kd, 6),\n        \"lambda\": round(lambda_c, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Kp = {Kp:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Ki = {Ki:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Kd = {Kd:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"Lambda (filter time constant) = {lambda_c:.4f} s\")\n\n    # Save tuned gains\n    with open('/root/tuned_gains.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(tuned_gains, f, indent=2)\n\n    return tuned_gains\n\n\ndef run_closed_loop_control(sim, tuned_gains, setpoint=22.0, duration=150.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run closed-loop temperature control.\n\n    Args:\n        sim: HVACSimulator instance (will be reset)\n        tuned_gains: Dictionary with Kp, Ki, Kd\n        setpoint: Target temperature (C)\n        duration: Control duration (seconds)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with control log\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"\\nRunning closed-loop control to {setpoint}C for {duration}s...\")\n\n    # Reset simulator to start fresh for control phase\n    initial_temp = sim.reset()\n    print(f\"Initial temperature: {initial_temp:.4f}C\")\n\n    # Initialize controller\n    controller = TemperatureController(\n        Kp=tuned_gains[\"Kp\"],\n        Ki=tuned_gains[\"Ki\"],\n        Kd=tuned_gains[\"Kd\"]\n    )\n\n    dt = sim.get_dt()\n    data = []\n\n    # Run control loop\n    steps = int(duration / dt)\n    current_temp = initial_temp\n\n    for i in range(steps):\n        # Compute control action\n        error = setpoint - current_temp\n        heater_power = controller.compute(error, dt)\n\n        # Apply control action\n        result = sim.step(heater_power)\n\n        current_temp = result[\"temperature\"]\n        current_time = result[\"time\"]\n\n        data.append({\n            \"time\": round(current_time, 2),\n            \"temperature\": round(current_temp, 4),\n            \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n            \"heater_power\": round(result[\"heater_power\"], 4),\n            \"error\": round(error, 4)\n        })\n\n    control_log = {\n        \"phase\": \"control\",\n        \"setpoint\": setpoint,\n        \"data\": data\n    }\n\n    # Save control log\n    with open('/root/control_log.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(control_log, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(f\"Control complete: {len(data)} data points\")\n    final_temp = data[-1][\"temperature\"]\n    final_error = abs(final_temp - setpoint)\n    print(f\"Final temperature: {final_temp:.4f}C (error: {final_error:.4f}C)\")\n\n    return control_log\n\n\ndef calculate_metrics(control_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate performance metrics from control log.\n\n    Args:\n        control_log: Dictionary with control data\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary with performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\nCalculating performance metrics...\")\n\n    data = control_log[\"data\"]\n    setpoint = control_log[\"setpoint\"]\n\n    times = np.array([d[\"time\"] for d in data])\n    temps = np.array([d[\"temperature\"] for d in data])\n    errors = np.array([d[\"error\"] for d in data])\n\n    # Initial temperature\n    T0 = temps[0]\n\n    # Rise time: time to go from 10% to 90% of setpoint change\n    delta_T = setpoint - T0\n    T_10 = T0 + 0.1 * delta_T\n    T_90 = T0 + 0.9 * delta_T\n\n    idx_10 = np.where(temps >= T_10)[0]\n    idx_90 = np.where(temps >= T_90)[0]\n\n    if len(idx_10) > 0 and len(idx_90) > 0:\n        rise_time = times[idx_90[0]] - times[idx_10[0]]\n    else:\n        rise_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Overshoot: maximum excursion beyond setpoint\n    max_temp = np.max(temps)\n    if max_temp > setpoint:\n        overshoot = (max_temp - setpoint) / abs(delta_T) if abs(delta_T) > 0 else 0.0\n    else:\n        overshoot = 0.0\n\n    # Settling time: time to stay within 2% of setpoint\n    settling_band = 0.02 * abs(delta_T)\n    settled = np.abs(errors) <= settling_band\n\n    if np.any(settled):\n        # Find first time it enters and stays in band\n        first_settled_idx = np.where(settled)[0][0]\n        # Check if it stays settled\n        if np.all(settled[first_settled_idx:]):\n            settling_time = times[first_settled_idx] - times[0]\n        else:\n            # Find last time it enters the band permanently\n            for i in range(len(settled) - 1, -1, -1):\n                if np.all(settled[i:]):\n                    settling_time = times[i] - times[0]\n                    break\n            else:\n                settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n    else:\n        settling_time = times[-1] - times[0]\n\n    # Steady-state error: average error over last 10% of data\n    steady_state_window = int(len(temps) * 0.1)\n    steady_state_error = np.mean(np.abs(errors[-steady_state_window:]))\n\n    metrics = {\n        \"rise_time\": round(rise_time, 4),\n        \"overshoot\": round(overshoot, 6),\n        \"settling_time\": round(settling_time, 4),\n        \"steady_state_error\": round(steady_state_error, 6),\n        \"max_temp\": round(max_temp, 4)\n    }\n\n    print(f\"Rise time: {rise_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Overshoot: {overshoot*100:.2f}%\")\n    print(f\"Settling time: {settling_time:.4f} s\")\n    print(f\"Steady-state error: {steady_state_error:.4f} C\")\n    print(f\"Max temperature: {max_temp:.4f} C\")\n\n    # Save metrics\n    with open('/root/metrics.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(metrics, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Check constraints\n    print(\"\\nConstraint Verification:\")\n    print(f\"  Steady-state error < 0.5C: {steady_state_error < 0.5} ({steady_state_error:.4f}C)\")\n    print(f\"  Settling time < 120s: {settling_time < 120} ({settling_time:.4f}s)\")\n    print(f\"  Overshoot < 10%: {overshoot < 0.10} ({overshoot*100:.2f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  Max temperature < 30C: {max_temp < 30.0} ({max_temp:.4f}C)\")\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"TEMPERATURE CONTROLLER SYSTEM\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    #
